[E16 MAIN RESULT id=6BPtJYvq] {"id": "page_qa_runbook", "space_id": "space_ENG", "parent_id": "page_eng_home", "title": "QA Regression Runbook", "body": "<p>Manual QA regression pass run against every release candidate before we submit a build for review. Owned by QA (Vincent Parker); anyone cutting a build can run it, but a green matrix here is a gate on the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:content-title=\"Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission\"/><ac:link-body>Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission</ac:link-body></ac:link>. Scope covers Domino Delights (internal codename GoD, \"Game of Dominoes\"), Zombie Match 3D (listed as \"Puzzles vs Zombies\" until the July 2025 rename). Budget a few hours per title - the first five chapters alone run over an hour; if something fails, file the bug and block the release, don't hand-wave it.</p><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\"note\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Test the UPDATE path, not just a fresh install.</strong> The June 2025 progression losses were on <strong>Domino Delights</strong>, and nobody ever reproduced them - the leading hypothesis was the update path, which is why we test it. Every candidate must be installed <em>over</em> the last two shipped versions and checked for save/progression loss before we ship. If we only test fresh installs we will miss this entire class of bug again.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Recurring bug classes to always check</h2><p>These four have all bitten us in production. Treat them as non-negotiable checks on every pass, on real devices, not just the emulator.</p><ul><li><strong>Font / asset download stall on splash.</strong> In May 2025 Domino Delights froze at roughly 22% on the splash screen while pulling remote fonts and assets (ENG-2346, build 0.2.197). Watch the loading bar all the way to gameplay; test on a throttled/slow connection and with the app cache cleared.</li><li><strong>Lives &amp; scheduler regeneration.</strong> Confirm lives tick back up on the timer, refill correctly after device clock changes, and that offline time is credited without over-granting.</li><li><strong>Quest population &amp; reset.</strong> New quests populate on a fresh session, complete correctly, and roll over at the daily/weekly reset without duplicating or leaving empty slots.</li><li><strong>Save / progression integrity.</strong> Level, coins and unlocks survive an update, a force-quit and a re-launch. This is the one the June 2025 wipe belongs to.</li></ul><h2>Regression matrix</h2><p>Run each row on at least one iOS and one Android device. Both update rows are mandatory - N-1 and N-2 are the last two shipped store versions.</p><table><tbody><tr><th>Area</th><th>Fresh install</th><th>Update from N-1</th><th>Update from N-2</th><th>Notes</th></tr><tr><td>Splash / asset load</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Watch for the ~22% stall (May 2025 Domino Delights)</td></tr><tr><td>Save / progression</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>June 2025 wipe never reproduced in-house; update path is the leading hypothesis</td></tr><tr><td>Lives &amp; scheduler</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Test with device clock changes</td></tr><tr><td>Quests populate / reset</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Check daily + weekly rollover</td></tr><tr><td>Purchase / restore</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Sandbox account. NOTE: entitlements do <strong>not</strong> restore on reinstall and receipts are not validated server-side - see the per-title IAP row below. Check the purchase grants client-side, not that it restores.</td></tr></tbody></table><h3>If a check fails</h3><ul><li>File the bug with device, OS version, source build and repro steps, and link it here.</li><li>A failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release - fresh-install-only regressions do not pass the gate.</li><li>Player-facing symptoms (lost progress, stuck splash, missing lives) should be mirrored to the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:co
...[TRUNCATED 4371 chars]...
nt-gen iPhone, latest OS</td><td>iPhone SE-class, oldest supported OS</td><td>iPad (one gen)</td></tr>\n<tr><td>Android</td><td>current-gen Samsung / Pixel</td><td>low-RAM device, oldest supported OS</td><td>one Android tablet</td></tr></tbody></table>\n<h3>Sign-off</h3><p>QA posts the completed matrix in <code>#god-bugs (Domino Delights) or #zombie-bugs (ZM3D)</code> and links it from the release ticket. A build without a green matrix should not get submitted. In practice Friday is the usual release day and builds have shipped without a completed pass - QA has asked for a Friday freeze but it has not been adopted.</p>", "body_format": "storage", "status": "current", "version": 2, "author_id": "user_EMPLOYEE_0025", "created_at": "2024-04-15T10:11:23Z", "updated_at": "2025-08-04T10:00:00Z"}

[E30 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=PCGSCudC] {"fileId": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59"}
[E31 MAIN RESULT id=PCGSCudC] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# ZM submission rules\nLeonard, Sep 23\n\nAfter the orange juice one on Sunday I want the gate tighter, not just the Bug view in Linear.\n\nScope: every ZM ticket that's still open (any state but Done, Canceled or Duplicate), whatever team or label (ART or DES tickets with ZM in the title count too), plus everything that moved to Done after the last gate's builds were cut (Android #127 / iOS #40 on Sep 22). Release tickets, ZOM-359 and ZOM-475 stay out, that work is separate. From the next gate on, tickets filed while the gate is running get a row too, including the ones you file yourself.\n\nA ticket counts as fixed when Vincent or Brian saw it working on the candidate builds, or on prod if the fix is server side. A Done ticket for a bug nobody has re-checked yet also counts as fixed if its fix is actually in the candidate builds (or live on prod), noted as not re-checked. Done in Linear or a dev saying fixed isn't enough by itself, and if the latest check on the candidates still shows the bug, call it on how bad it is whatever the ticket says.\n\nBlocker = crash, freeze, player stuck or can't finish a level (like the orange juice one), lost progress / coins / lives (infinite-lives time included) / boosters / inventory items, or a purchase that doesn't deliver. Any other bug a player can see is a known issue, even if it doesn't change play, visual and sound glitches included. Art/VFX requests, requests for design, balance or tuning changes, analytics, tools, backend endpoints and new features aren't bugs, no call. The same goes for a ticket that only asks for new or updated art, animation, VFX, text style or UI layout (replacing an outdated model or icon with its new version included), even with a Bug label and even once it's Done, and for a ticket that doesn't say what goes wrong.\n\nBlockers go on the release ticket. If in doubt ask me before the go/no-go, not after.\n", "id": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59", "name": "ZM submission rules.docx"}

[E32 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=iX5MVbRd] {"fileId": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95"}
[E33 MAIN RESULT id=iX5MVbRd] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# How ZM submission builds are cut\nArthur, Sep 24\n\nSo nobody has to guess when gating:\n\n- Since the hotfix train ended on Sep 3, every store submission (the Match3D Android Submission and Match 3D iOS Submission jobs) is cut straight from main. The auth branch submission jobs are sign-in test builds, not store candidates.\n- A submission has exactly what was on main when its job started (the Started line in #builds) and nothing that landed after, even if the build finishes an hour later. Don't go by the Changes block either: the iOS posts only ever print the newest change, Android prints the whole list.\n- Backend is separate: match3d-backend changes reach players only when Douglas deploys the backend to prod, by hand (see the CI/CD page, CI stops at the build). Merged is not live.\n", "id": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95", "name": "How ZM submission builds are cut.docx"}

[E34 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=sUoaSjRz] {"fileId": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412"}
[E35 MAIN RESULT id=sUoaSjRz] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\nBrian Foster, Sep 30 2025 (after the fresh-install pass)\n\nUpdate rows from the QA regression runbook. Fresh-install rows are in my regression matrix sheet.\n\nDevice | OS | Installed from store | Updated to | Result | Notes\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-1 | iOS Submission #50 | FAIL | After the update the save came back at level 1 with 500 coins; the account was on level 212 with 18,400 coins before. Relaunching didn't bring it back.\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-2 | iOS Submission #50 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-1 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-2 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\n\nRepro for the iOS row: install the N-1 build from the App Store, play past level 200, install TestFlight 50 over it, launch.\n", "id": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412", "name": "Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135.docx"}

[E239 MAIN RESULT id=k2y8aNZA] {"id": "ZOM-481", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-481", "number": 481, "title": "Zombie Health bar persists too long after death.", "description": "When the zombies die, their health bar is still present after a sec.  \n\nThe video below is in 0.3x. It shows that the health bar can clutter and cause visual noise. \n\nHowever, as you can notice in the video, it might also cause mistargets. If the items are matched fast enough, the first object matched after a zombie is killed can still target the dead zombie. Therefore, some items can be used for nothing which can also prevents balancing the board population. \n\n[persistent health bar - Made with Clipchamp.mp4]([URL_52796])", "priority": {"value": 1, "name": "Urgent"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-481/zombie-health-bar-persists-too-long-after-death", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-481-zombie-health-bar-persists-too-long-after-death", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T18:02:51Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-30T22:10:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Bug"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "owen.baker@harmonygames.co", "assigneeId": "usr_4e13bd40cc73d0fc", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 68460, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E328 MAIN RESULT id=GAb1eQbG] {"id": "ZOM-237", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-237", "number": 237, "title": "packs animations bugs", "description": "[[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6254].0999997.mp4]([URL_52578])\n\nThe new banner animation plays twice per card. if you look for example at 00:23 when the card flips from the back to front, the new red banner its already on there and after it starts the animation again. \n\nI think we should move the banner and its pop up dotween animation into your card prefab and not let my card animation have it. I dont know how you controll the new banner to appear.\n\nSFX problems:\n\nBecause i dont know how you control the multiplier and new banner to appear when i added sfx to them the sfx still play in the background but if the banner doesnt exist or the multiplier text doesnt appear they still play in the background. [E355 MAIN RESULT id=fApuspBW] {"id": "ZOM-484", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-484", "number": 484, "title": "Info Screen Animations", "description": "We will add animations to the infographic tutorials we use in Domino Delights, like in the references\n\n* We will animate the parts in each infographic tutorial in order\n* All other parts will be animated in groups\n* The animation will be used as a template and will be used in all other infographics\n  * Since Leaderboard's infographic consists of different groups in one piece, we can make a differe", "priority": {"value": 4, "name": "Low"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-484/info-screen-animations", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-484-info-screen-animations", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T19:15:03Z", "updatedAt": "2025-08-14T19:15:03Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": [], "links": [], "attachments": [{"id": "a8a542228361f6109f8654ce", "title": "ZOM-484__2eec390a-51ee-46.png", "subtitle": null, "url": "https://uploads.linear.app/a32d013e-0a11-5c58-ab4d-cd09016f571f/419a06d5-ead5-56b1-abab-f0489724586a/4a287863-b267-55bb-9654-af699a5c29ad"}], "createdBy": "brian.foster@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_fc22d5941b2aeb5c", "assignee": "marcus.bennett@harmonygames.co", "assigneeId": "usr_c77c50cc15c5342d", "project": "Zombie Match 3D", "projectId": "dbab77c7-f871-4888-abf8-d98b52a41624", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 68389, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": [], "descriptionTruncated": true, "descriptionLength": 812}

[E356 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_get_issue id=m8K8uoLH] {"id": "ZOM-477", "maxDescriptionChars": 600}
[E357 MAIN RESULT id=m8K8uoLH] {"id": "ZOM-477", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-477", "number": 477, "title": "DOT kills can take too long and cause perceived unresponsivenes", "description": "**When the kill cam is triggered, the game is slowed down. That causes some UX issues when zombies are killed by DOT, especially if they will be dead for the last damage by DOT.** \n\n[Record_[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6338].mp4]([URL_52793])\n\nThere are couple of ways to go around\n\n* If the kill cam is not triggered by an item thrown, don't make the sequence too slowed down. \n* Don't use more than 2 seconds of DOT\n* or implement the DOT death VFXs and make the death instant rather than counting down the life.", "priority": {"value": 3, "name": "Medium"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-477/dot-kills-can-take-too-long-and-cause-perceived-unresponsivenes", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-477-dot-kills-can-take-too-long-and-cause-perceived", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T11:51:12Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-24T09:20:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Improvement"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "EMPLOYEE_0032_NAME", "assigneeId": "usr_EMPLOYEE_0032", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 68832, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E380 MAIN RESULT id=Yi3C1ftZ] {"id": "ZOM-473", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-473", "number": 473, "title": "Endless mode ending screen can be skipped after death", "description": "When the endless mode is over, players can still try to match items with urgency but as soon as the level is over, the ending screen is visible.\n\nHowever that screen has no input blocker whatsoever. So it might be skipped at once while trying to match objects. \n\nHere is the expected outcome:\n\n* When the level ends, block the input until the x2 multiplication happens. So that players won't skip it accidentally. \n\n[[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6336].8623973.mp4]([URL_52789])", "priority": {"value": 3, "name": "Medium"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-473/endless-mode-ending-screen-can-be-skipped-after-death", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-473-endless-mode-ending-screen-can-be-skipped-after-death", "createdAt": "2025-08-14T08:24:51Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-22T15:11:51Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": "2025-09-22T15:11:51Z", "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Done", "statusType": "completed", "labels": ["Bug"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "EMPLOYEE_0032_NAME", "assigneeId": "usr_EMPLOYEE_0032", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 12472, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": []}

[E424 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_create_issue id=CnsAXtZy] {"team": "Zombie Match 3D", "title": "Updating iOS from the N-1 store build to iOS Submission #50 resets the save to level 1", "description": "Found by the QA regression runbook's update-path check on the ZOM-570 store candidates (Brian's run: \"Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\").\n\n**What happens**\nOn iPhone 13 / iOS 18.6, installing iOS Submission #50 (TestFlight 50) over the N-1 App Store build brings the save back at **level 1 with 500 coins**. Before the update the account was on **level 212 with 18,400 coins**. Relaunching doesn't restore it.\n\n**Repro**\n1. Install the N-1 build from the App Store.\n2. Play past level 200.\n3. Install TestFlight 50 (iOS Submission #50) over it.\n4. Launch.\n\n**Expected:** level, coins and unlocks carry over the update, as they do in the other update rows.\n\n**Other update rows in the same run (all passed: level, coins and unlocks kept)**\n* iPhone 13, iOS N-2 → #50\n* Pixel 6 / Android 14, N-1 → Android Submission #135\n* Pixel 6 / Android 14, N-2 → Android Submission #135\n\n**Device:** iPhone 13, iOS 18.6\n**Source build:** N-1 App Store build → iOS Submission #50\n\nPer the runbook this counts as save/progression loss in an UPDATE column, so it blocks the release. It's on ZOM-570 as a blocker.", "priority": 1, "labels": ["Bug"], "relatedTo": ["ZOM-570"]}
[E425 MAIN RESULT id=CnsAXtZy] {"id": "ZOM-581", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-581", "number": 581, "title": "Updating iOS from the N-1 store build to iOS Submission #50 resets the save to level 1", "description": "Found by the QA regression runbook's update-path check on the ZOM-570 store candidates (Brian's run: \"Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\").\n\n**What happens**\nOn iPhone 13 / iOS 18.6, installing iOS Submission #50 (TestFlight 50) over the N-1 App Store build brings the save back at **level 1 with 500 coins**. [E453 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_update_issue id=h5MF5bgk] {"id": "ZOM-570", "patch": [{"op": "insert_before", "anchor": "Other push notes go in the comments here.", "text": "## QA gate blockers (Android #135 / iOS #50) - Vincent, Oct 1\n\nQA call: **NO-GO**. 14 blockers. Full register is in the \"Sep 30 - Android #135 / iOS #50\" tab of the ZM3D Release Gate sheet.\n\n* **ZOM-581** - iOS update from the N-1 store build to #50 resets the save to level 1 (runbook update-path row FAILED, new ticket)\n* **ZOM-435** - stuck on trophy path after a fast double claim, have to kill the app (Leonard repro on #135)\n* **ZOM-567** - Zombie Lane chests don't land in the inventory on prod (server - not live: #36 is deployed, but its chest-rows data load hasn't been run on prod)\n* **ZOM-569** - Season Pass from the join popup leaves an empty screen, have to kill the app (fix landed after the Started line, not in the candidates)\n* **ZOM-449** - low-end Android: cold start into level 3 freezes until force-quit\n* **ZOM-578** - fresh install stuck at tutorial step 3\n* **ZOM-483** - new-install infinite lives reset to 30 min on relaunch (time lost)\n* **ZOM-560** - abandoned pack purchase doesn't deliver the win streak freezer time\n* **ZOM-554** - quitting mid tornado leaves the next level unplayable until force-quit\n* **ZOM-546** - endless refill drains the board, run can't go on\n* **ZOM-576** - Season Pass premium tier 12 claim drops the booster bundle\n* **ZOM-573** - Collect&Win: claiming the 5th chest crashes the app\n* **ZOM-559** - Samsung home-out: victory screen freezes until force-quit (latest check on #133, not re-tested on #135)\n* **DES-313** - level 214 can't be completed (only 2 of 3 jars spawn)\n\n"}]}

[E464 MAIN ASSISTANT-TEXT]
I ran the QA gate on Android Submission #135 and iOS Submission #50, and the call is **no-go** for Friday: 14 blockers. The register, the release ticket, the new ticket and the Slack reply are all done.

**Blockers** (now listed by key in ZOM-570's description):
- **ZOM-581 (new):** Brian's update test from the runbook failed on iOS. Installing #50 over the previous store version brought the save back at level 1 with 500 coins, down from level 212 and 18,400 coins. The runbook says any failure on an update path blocks the release. Nobody had filed it, so this is the only ticket I opened.
- **ZOM-435:** the trophy path gets stuck again on #135 if you tap claim twice fast (Leonard's note on the Trello tech-issues card). I put this under the existing ticket rather than opening a duplicate, per Arthur's reuse rule.
- **ZOM-567:** closed in Linear, but not fixed for players. Backend PR #36 is deployed to prod, but its chest data hasn't been loaded there. Arthur claimed a chest on prod afterwards and nothing landed, and he's holding the data load until after the store push.
- **ZOM-569:** the Season Pass fix went into main at 21:36Z. The builds started at 18:43Z (iOS) and 18:45Z (Android), so it isn't in either candidate.
- **The other ten are open bugs that still happen:** ZOM-449, 578, 483, 560, 554, 546, 576, 573, 559 and DES-313. They are freezes, crashes, players stuck, lost infinite-lives time, boosters lost or purchases not delivering.
- **One weaker call:** ZOM-559 (Samsung freeze after leaving and returning to the app) hasn't been re-tested on #135. I called it a blocker because the latest check, on build #133, froze.

**Register:** a new "Sep 30 - Android #135 / iOS #50" tab in your ZM3D Release Gate sheet, 133 tickets with evidence for each:
- 14 blockers
- 22 known issues
- 11 fixed. Of those, 7 were closed before the builds started but nobody has re-checked them yet: ZOM-557, 473, 503, 558, 506, 434 and 561.
- 86 no call (art, VFX, analytics, tools, features)

**Judgement calls worth a look before Friday:**
- I called ZOM-562 (single leftover items in the boss arena) a known issue, since nothing shows the level can't be finished.
- I called ZOM-427 (Android app icon too zoomed in) a known issue rather than an art request.
- I counted ZOM-563 (team lives, frames and names on prod) as fixed based on your "works as expected" DM to Douglas.

**Slack:** I replied in Arthur's candidates thread in #zombie-bugs with the call and the blocker list. My first reply got the breakdown of the 11 fixed tickets wrong, so I posted a short correction straight after. The blockers and the call weren't affected.
